# All closed Android pull requests: exhaustive historical audit

## Scope, evidence, and limits
This audit covers **all 2442 closed pull requests** returned by GitHub for `kiwix/kiwix-android`: **2037 merged and 405 closed-unmerged**. A closed-unmerged proposal is abandoned/unmerged evidence, not a shipped fix. Creation dates span **2017-03-18 through 2026-10-28**. The snapshot was retrieved on 2026-10-31; exact timestamps are in [android-all-closed-prs.raw.json.xz](android-all-closed-prs.raw.json.xz). Open PRs are outside the requested closed-PR scope.
**Skipped as noise: 260 of 2442** — 140 translations-only (translatewiki/Weblate or localization-only title), 94 CI/build/dependency/release-automation chores, 26 test-only changes. No dependency-bot PRs exist in this repository. A PR that names an issue is never skipped merely because its title also mentions tooling. The remaining **2182 PRs** (1813 merged, 369 closed-unmerged) are classified below.
Evidence is the original GitHub PR-list objects: title, body, author, labels, timestamps and `merged_at`. Diffs were **not** fetched for this audit; where a PR body links an issue, the disposition is inherited from the issue audit, not from an inspected patch. This is a deliberate scope limit: the deliverable is parity with upstream-fixed behaviour, and the issue audit carries the behavioural analysis. Titles can overstate scope; a merged PR title is not proof that every promised case was implemented.
No build, test, formatter or runtime check was executed. No source file was changed.

| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| audited (all) | 13 | 45 | 1697 | 427 | 2182 |
| merged | 13 | 42 | 1385 | 373 | 1813 |
| closed-unmerged | 0 | 3 | 312 | 54 | 369 |
| skipped noise | — | — | — | — | 260 |

## Highest-signal PR groups
These groups are where a merged upstream PR encodes a contract we must not regress. The linked issue rows carry the file references.

| Group | Representative PRs | Contract |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Storage / split archives / provider access | [#3691](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/3691), [#4510](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4510), [#3605](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/3605), [#3861](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/3861) | Exact `.zimaa` cohort is one book; no partial admission; retained provider descriptors. |
| Downloads / resume / notification | [#4140](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4140), [#4138](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4138), [#4748](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4748) | Durable jobs, verified publication, explicit pause/resume; reconnect policy must be stated. |
| Reader URL/path encoding | [#5188](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5188), [#3755](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/3755), [#4954](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4954) | Percent-encode locations and OPDS query parameters; never form-decode `+`. |
| Notes / bookmarks / history identity | [#5164](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5164), [#5168](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5168), [#5176](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5176), [#5181](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5181), [#5149](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5149) | Identity is archive + full path; deletion is row-scoped. |
| Catalogue pagination / empty state | [#4701](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4701), [#5011](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5011), [#5118](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5118) | Offsets/filters preserved; explicit empty/error states; sync is a separate contract. |
| Dark theme / appearance | [#5132](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5132), [#4813](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4813), [#4814](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4814) | Archive content must be restyled without inverting images; no launch flash. |
| TOC / find-in-page | [#5207](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/5207), [#4200](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4200), [#4377](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/4377) | Empty-outline control disabled; find scoped to the active tab. |
| Legacy service workers | [#3234](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/3234), [#3854](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/pull/3854) | Upstream never delivered a working replay path; our disclosure-only stance is not a regression. |

## Skipped PRs
| Category | Count | Why skipped |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| translation | 140 | translatewiki/Weblate or localization-only; translated strings are locale-audit work, not behaviour. |
| ci-build | 94 | CI, Gradle/NDK/dependency bumps, lint/proguard/signing, release uploads; no reader contract. |
| tests | 26 | Test-only changes; no product behaviour. |
| dependency bots | 0 | No dependabot/renovate PRs were returned. |

## Non-fixes, reverts and misleading chronology
- **Closed-unmerged is not a fix.** Every unmerged row is labelled above. Do not treat a proposal's title as delivered behaviour.
- **Dependency/toolchain bumps are not behaviour.** `libkiwix`/`libzim` upgrades can change archive compatibility, but a version bump alone does not prove a rendering or search fix; the issue audit keeps those rows `unverified` where no fixture exists.
- **Custom-app and Play Store packaging PRs** affect branded distribution, not the standard reader; they are `not-applicable` unless they also change shared code.
- **Translation PRs** do not establish locale completeness; that is owned by the locale audit.
- **Reverts** are not individually inferred without diffs; a later revert could invalidate an earlier merged PR. The issue audit is the authoritative behavioural map, and it names the surviving contract.

## What could not be determined
- No PR diffs were inspected, so a merged PR whose body overstates its change is recorded at title/body level. This is sufficient to map *which upstream behaviours were fixed*, not to certify the patch.
- PRs closed without an issue link and without a recognizable subsystem title fall to `unverified` or `not-applicable` by rule; the raw artifact retains their original text for re-triage.
- The listing is a snapshot; comments, reviews and later reverts were not fetched. Merged status is authoritative at retrieval time.
